# ==============================================================================
#  大炎·珍玩集册 —— 引擎
#
#  变量：
#    YAN_col_index        1..294   图鉴当前浏览的收藏品序号
#    YAN_col_cur_tier     0..4     当前件的品阶（浏览时由 Refresh_Cursor 写入）
#    YAN_col_cur_rar      0..2     当前件的稀有度
#    YAN_col_cur_owned    0/1      当前件是否已收藏
#    YAN_col_cur_cat      1..9     当前件所属分类（左侧页签高亮用）
#    YAN_col_total        0..294   已收藏件数
#    YAN_col_draw_cat     0..9     下次寻珍的品类（0 = 全品类）—— 跟随当前浏览的件
#    YAN_col_pick         1..294   本次抽中的序号
#    YAN_col_filter_mode  0=全部 / 1=只看未收藏 / 2=只看可升阶（上/下一件按它跳）
#    YAN_col_scan          循环计数（找下一件时的保护上限）
#    YAN_col_result_kind  0=新收藏 / 1=升阶 / 2=空手
#    YAN_col_result_open  0/1      结果卡是否展示
#  旗标 / 逐件变量：
#    YAN_col_<id>_owned            是否已收藏
#    YAN_col_<id>_tier             品阶 1..4（未收藏时不设置）
#  由 tools（E:/tmp/yan_col/gen_effects.js）生成，手改会被覆盖
# ==============================================================================

YAN_Collection_Init_Variables = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index > 0 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_index = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_total > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_total = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_total_i > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_total_i = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n1 > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n1 = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n2 > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n2 = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n3 > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n3 = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n4 > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n4 = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n1_i > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n1_i = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n2_i > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n2_i = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n3_i > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n3_i = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_n4_i > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_n4_i = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_cur_tier > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_tier = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_cur_rar > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_rar = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_cur_owned > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_owned = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_cur_cat > 0 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_cat = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_draw_cat > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_draw_cat = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_pick > 0 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_pick = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_result_open > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_result_open = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_filter_mode > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_filter_mode = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_col_scan > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_col_scan = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_attack > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_attack = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_defence > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_defence = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_org > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_org = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_exp > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_exp = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_training > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_training = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_conscription > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_conscription = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_industrial > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_industrial = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_production > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_production = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_research > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_research = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_stability > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_stability = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_factor > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_factor = 0 } }

	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_defence_f > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_army_defence_f = 0 } }

	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_training_i > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_training_i = 0 } }

	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_stability_i > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_stability_i = 0 } }

	if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_f > -1 } } } set_variable = { YAN_bonus_pp_gain_f = 0 } }

	# 打开面板时刷新加成汇总（防止外部途径注入/历史继承的收藏品未计入）
	YAN_Collection_Recalc_Bonus = yes
}

YAN_Collection_Unlock = {
	YAN_Collection_Init_Variables = yes
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = YAN_col_unlocked } }
		set_country_flag = YAN_col_unlocked
		country_event = { id = yan_collection.1 days = 2 }
	}
}

# ---------- 开关面板 ----------
YAN_Collection_Open = {
	YAN_Collection_Init_Variables = yes
	set_country_flag = YAN_col_open
	set_variable = { YAN_col_result_open = 0 }
	YAN_Collection_Refresh_Cursor = yes
}

YAN_Collection_Close = {
	clr_country_flag = YAN_col_open
	set_variable = { YAN_col_result_open = 0 }
}

YAN_Collection_Close_Result = {
	set_variable = { YAN_col_result_open = 0 }
}

# ---------- 视图切换：图鉴单品浏览 <-> 集册总加成 ----------
# 两个窗口用 YAN_col_view 互斥，不会叠窗，也就不必给几十个控件逐个加显隐触发器
YAN_Collection_Show_Summary = {
	YAN_Collection_Init_Variables = yes
	YAN_Collection_Recalc_Bonus = yes
	set_variable = { YAN_col_view = 1 }
	set_country_flag = YAN_col_open
	set_variable = { YAN_col_result_open = 0 }
}

YAN_Collection_Show_Item = {
	set_variable = { YAN_col_view = 0 }
}

# ---------- 光标：把当前件的品阶/稀有度/归属写进 YAN_col_cur_* ----------
YAN_Collection_Refresh_Cursor = {
	set_variable = { YAN_col_view = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_rar = 2 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_cat = 1 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_draw_cat = 1 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_owned = 0 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_tier = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = YAN_col_001_owned }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_owned = 1 }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_tier = YAN_col_001_tier }
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_rar = 0 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_cat = 1 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_draw_cat = 1 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_owned = 0 }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_tier = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = YAN_col_002_owned }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_owned = 1 }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_tier = YAN_col_002_tier }
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_rar = 0 }

			set_variable = { YAN_col_cur_tier = YAN_col_294_tier }
		}
	}
}

# 上一件 / 下一件：受 YAN_col_filter_mode 影响（全部 = 逐件，未收藏/可升阶 = 跳到最近一件）
YAN_Collection_Prev = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index > 1 } } add_to_variable = { YAN_col_index = -1 } else = { set_variable = { YAN_col_index = 294 } } }
	set_variable = { YAN_col_scan = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { YAN_col_scan < 400 }
			NOT = { YAN_col_cur_matches_filter = yes }
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index > 1 } } add_to_variable = { YAN_col_index = -1 } else = { set_variable = { YAN_col_index = 294 } } }
		add_to_variable = { YAN_col_scan = 1 }
	}
	YAN_Collection_Refresh_Cursor = yes
}

YAN_Collection_Next = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index < 294 } } add_to_variable = { YAN_col_index = 1 } else = { set_variable = { YAN_col_index = 1 } } }
	set_variable = { YAN_col_scan = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { YAN_col_scan < 400 }
			NOT = { YAN_col_cur_matches_filter = yes }
		}
		add_to_variable = { YAN_col_index = 1 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_index > 294 } } set_variable = { YAN_col_index = 1 } }
		add_to_variable = { YAN_col_scan = 1 }
	}
	YAN_Collection_Refresh_Cursor = yes
}

# 筛选开关：切模式后跳到最近一件符合的（当前件已符合则原地不动）

# 寻珍：按 YAN_col_draw_cat 从对应品类里随机取一件「未集齐」的（含可升阶的）

	set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 2 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_draw_cat = 0 compare = equals } YAN_col_any_candidate = yes }
			random_list = {
				1 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 has_country_flag = YAN_col_001_owned check_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 4 compare = equals } }
					set_variable = { YAN_col_pick = 1 }

# 结算：YAN_col_pick 指定的一件 → 未收藏则收入（1 档），已收藏则升一档（最高史前）
YAN_Collection_Grant = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_pick = 1 compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = YAN_col_001_owned } }
			set_country_flag = YAN_col_001_owned
			set_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 1 }
			add_ideas = YAN_col_001_1
			add_to_variable = { YAN_col_total = 1 }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 0 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 2 }
			remove_ideas = YAN_col_001_1
			add_ideas = YAN_col_001_2
			set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 1 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 3 }
			remove_ideas = YAN_col_001_2
			add_ideas = YAN_col_001_3
			set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 1 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 3 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_001_tier = 4 }
			remove_ideas = YAN_col_001_3
			add_ideas = YAN_col_001_4
			set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_pick = 2 compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = YAN_col_002_owned } }
			set_country_flag = YAN_col_002_owned
			set_variable = { YAN_col_002_tier = 1 }
			add_ideas = YAN_col_002_1
			add_to_variable = { YAN_col_total = 1 }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 0 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_002_tier = 1 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_002_tier = 2 }
			remove_ideas = YAN_col_002_1
			add_ideas = YAN_col_002_2
			set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 1 }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { YAN_col_002_tier = 2 compare = equals } }
			set_variable = { YAN_col_002_tier = 3 }
			remove_ideas = YAN_col_002_2
			add_ideas = YAN_col_002_3
			set_variable = { YAN_col_result_kind = 1 }
		}
